codex
Profiling showed the all-pairs arithmetic—not device memory bandwidth—is dominant on this FP64 workload. A warp-sized tile performs better than a conventional 256-thread tile on the available Ampere GPU because it avoids whole-block synchronization and leaves the scheduler more independent work. I’m refining that into warp-private shared tiles inside larger blocks, which should retain full occupancy without reintroducing cross-warp barriers.
apply patch

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+inline void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        cudaFailure(status, operation);
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 128;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+
+// Each warp streams a coalesced position tile through a private shared-memory
+// segment.  Warp-level barriers avoid coupling independently progressing warps,
+// while shared-memory broadcasts reuse every source load for 32 target bodies.
+// Positions are written to a different allocation, so every force in a step
+// observes exactly the same pre-integration snapshot.
+template <int BLOCK_SIZE>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_SIZE)
+void advanceBodies(const double4* __restrict__ oldPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ newPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ velocities,
+                   const int numBodies) {
+    __shared__ double4 positionTile[BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int threadIndex = threadIdx.x;
+    const int warpLane = threadIndex % WARP_SIZE;
+    double4* const warpTile =
+        positionTile + (threadIndex / WARP_SIZE) * WARP_SIZE;
+    const int bodyIndex = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIndex;
+    const bool active = bodyIndex < numBodies;
+    const double4 ownPosition = active ? oldPositions[bodyIndex]
+                                       : make_double4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
+
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += WARP_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + warpLane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            warpTile[warpLane] = oldPositions[sourceIndex];
+        }
+        __syncwarp();
+
+        const int tileCount = min(WARP_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+#pragma unroll 8
+        for (int source = 0; source < tileCount; ++source) {
+            const double4 otherPosition = warpTile[source];
+            const double dx = otherPosition.x - ownPosition.x;
+            const double dy = otherPosition.y - ownPosition.y;
+            const double dz = otherPosition.z - ownPosition.z;
+            const double distanceSquared =
+                dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+            // CUDA's correctly rounded reciprocal-square-root avoids issuing
+            // separate square-root and division instructions.
+            const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+            const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+            forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncwarp();
     }
+
+    if (active) {
+        double4 velocity = velocities[bodyIndex];
+        velocity.x += DT * forceX;
+        velocity.y += DT * forceY;
+        velocity.z += DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocities[bodyIndex] = velocity;
+        newPositions[bodyIndex] =
+            make_double4(ownPosition.x + velocity.x * DT,
+                         ownPosition.y + velocity.y * DT,
+                         ownPosition.z + velocity.z * DT,
+                         0.0);
+    }
 }

+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : numBodies_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            positions[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].pos.x, bodies[i].pos.y,
+                                        bodies[i].pos.z, 0.0);
+            velocities[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].vel.x, bodies[i].vel.y,
+                                         bodies[i].vel.z, 0.0);
+        }
+
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[0], bytes), "position allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[1], bytes), "position scratch allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&velocities_, bytes), "velocity allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_[0], positions.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial positions to the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, velocities.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial velocities to the GPU");
+
+        // Force lazy CUDA module loading to happen during backend setup rather
+        // than on the first timed simulation step.  With zero bodies the
+        // kernel only traverses its synchronization-safe inactive path.
+        advanceBodies<CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE><<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            positions_[0], positions_[1], velocities_, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "warming up the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "warming up the CUDA device");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(positions_[0]);
+        cudaFree(positions_[1]);
+        cudaFree(velocities_);
+    }
+
+    void run(const int numSteps) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0 || numSteps == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int gridSize =
+            (numBodies_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            advanceBodies<CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE><<<gridSize, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+                positions_[currentPositions_],
+                positions_[currentPositions_ ^ 1], velocities_, numBodies_);
+            currentPositions_ ^= 1;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the n-body simulation");
+    }
+
+    void copyToHost(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions.data(), positions_[currentPositions_],
+                             bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final positions from the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities.data(), velocities_, bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final velocities from the GPU");
+

     }

+    double4* velocities_ = nullptr;

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+inline void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        cudaFailure(status, operation);
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 128;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+
+// Each warp streams a coalesced position tile through a private shared-memory
+// segment.  Warp-level barriers avoid coupling independently progressing warps,
+// while shared-memory broadcasts reuse every source load for 32 target bodies.
+// Positions are written to a different allocation, so every force in a step
+// observes exactly the same pre-integration snapshot.
+template <int BLOCK_SIZE>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_SIZE)
+void advanceBodies(const double4* __restrict__ oldPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ newPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ velocities,
+                   const int numBodies) {
+    __shared__ double4 positionTile[BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int threadIndex = threadIdx.x;
+    const int warpLane = threadIndex % WARP_SIZE;
+    double4* const warpTile =
+        positionTile + (threadIndex / WARP_SIZE) * WARP_SIZE;
+    const int bodyIndex = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIndex;
+    const bool active = bodyIndex < numBodies;
+    const double4 ownPosition = active ? oldPositions[bodyIndex]
+                                       : make_double4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
+
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += WARP_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + warpLane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            warpTile[warpLane] = oldPositions[sourceIndex];
+        }
+        __syncwarp();
+
+        const int tileCount = min(WARP_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+#pragma unroll 8
+        for (int source = 0; source < tileCount; ++source) {
+            const double4 otherPosition = warpTile[source];
+            const double dx = otherPosition.x - ownPosition.x;
+            const double dy = otherPosition.y - ownPosition.y;
+            const double dz = otherPosition.z - ownPosition.z;
+            const double distanceSquared =
+                dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+            // CUDA's correctly rounded reciprocal-square-root avoids issuing
+            // separate square-root and division instructions.
+            const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+            const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+            forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncwarp();
     }
+
+    if (active) {
+        double4 velocity = velocities[bodyIndex];
+        velocity.x += DT * forceX;
+        velocity.y += DT * forceY;
+        velocity.z += DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocities[bodyIndex] = velocity;
+        newPositions[bodyIndex] =
+            make_double4(ownPosition.x + velocity.x * DT,
+                         ownPosition.y + velocity.y * DT,
+                         ownPosition.z + velocity.z * DT,
+                         0.0);
+    }
 }

-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
-            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
-            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
-            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+inline void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        cudaFailure(status, operation);
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+
+// Each warp streams a coalesced position tile through a private shared-memory
+// segment.  Warp-level barriers avoid coupling independently progressing warps,
+// while shared-memory broadcasts reuse every source load for 32 target bodies.
+// Positions are written to a different allocation, so every force in a step
+// observes exactly the same pre-integration snapshot.
+template <int BLOCK_SIZE>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_SIZE)
+void advanceBodies(const double4* __restrict__ oldPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ newPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ velocities,
+                   const int numBodies) {
+    __shared__ double4 positionTile[BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int threadIndex = threadIdx.x;
+    const int warpLane = threadIndex % WARP_SIZE;
+    double4* const warpTile =
+        positionTile + (threadIndex / WARP_SIZE) * WARP_SIZE;
+    const int bodyIndex = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIndex;
+    const bool active = bodyIndex < numBodies;
+    const double4 ownPosition = active ? oldPositions[bodyIndex]
+                                       : make_double4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
+
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += WARP_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + warpLane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            warpTile[warpLane] = oldPositions[sourceIndex];
+        }
+        __syncwarp();
+
+        const int tileCount = min(WARP_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+#pragma unroll 8
+        for (int source = 0; source < tileCount; ++source) {
+            const double4 otherPosition = warpTile[source];
+            const double dx = otherPosition.x - ownPosition.x;
+            const double dy = otherPosition.y - ownPosition.y;
+            const double dz = otherPosition.z - ownPosition.z;
+            const double distanceSquared =
+                dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+            // CUDA's correctly rounded reciprocal-square-root avoids issuing
+            // separate square-root and division instructions.
+            const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+            const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+            forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncwarp();
     }
+
+    if (active) {
+        double4 velocity = velocities[bodyIndex];
+        velocity.x += DT * forceX;
+        velocity.y += DT * forceY;
+        velocity.z += DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocities[bodyIndex] = velocity;
+        newPositions[bodyIndex] =
+            make_double4(ownPosition.x + velocity.x * DT,
+                         ownPosition.y + velocity.y * DT,
+                         ownPosition.z + velocity.z * DT,
+                         0.0);
+    }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : numBodies_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            positions[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].pos.x, bodies[i].pos.y,
+                                        bodies[i].pos.z, 0.0);
+            velocities[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].vel.x, bodies[i].vel.y,
+                                         bodies[i].vel.z, 0.0);
+        }
+
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[0], bytes), "position allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[1], bytes), "position scratch allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&velocities_, bytes), "velocity allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_[0], positions.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial positions to the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, velocities.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial velocities to the GPU");
+
+        // Force lazy CUDA module loading to happen during backend setup rather
+        // than on the first timed simulation step.  With zero bodies the
+        // kernel only traverses its synchronization-safe inactive path.
+        advanceBodies<CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE><<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            positions_[0], positions_[1], velocities_, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "warming up the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "warming up the CUDA device");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(positions_[0]);
+        cudaFree(positions_[1]);
+        cudaFree(velocities_);
+    }
+
+    void run(const int numSteps) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0 || numSteps == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int gridSize =
+            (numBodies_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            advanceBodies<CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE><<<gridSize, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+                positions_[currentPositions_],
+                positions_[currentPositions_ ^ 1], velocities_, numBodies_);
+            currentPositions_ ^= 1;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the n-body simulation");
+    }
+
+    void copyToHost(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions.data(), positions_[currentPositions_],
+                             bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final positions from the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities.data(), velocities_, bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final velocities from the GPU");
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(positions[i].x, positions[i].y, positions[i].z);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(velocities[i].x, velocities[i].y,
+                                 velocities[i].z);
+        }
     }
-}
 
+  private:
+    int numBodies_ = 0;
+    int currentPositions_ = 0;
+    double4* positions_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    double4* velocities_ = nullptr;
+};

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+inline void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        cudaFailure(status, operation);
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+
+// Each warp streams a coalesced position tile through a private shared-memory
+// segment.  Warp-level barriers avoid coupling independently progressing warps,
+// while shared-memory broadcasts reuse every source load for 32 target bodies.
+// Positions are written to a different allocation, so every force in a step
+// observes exactly the same pre-integration snapshot.
+template <int BLOCK_SIZE>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_SIZE)
+void advanceBodies(const double4* __restrict__ oldPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ newPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ velocities,
+                   const int numBodies) {
+    __shared__ double4 positionTile[BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int threadIndex = threadIdx.x;
+    const int warpLane = threadIndex % WARP_SIZE;
+    double4* const warpTile =
+        positionTile + (threadIndex / WARP_SIZE) * WARP_SIZE;
+    const int bodyIndex = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIndex;
+    const bool active = bodyIndex < numBodies;
+    const double4 ownPosition = active ? oldPositions[bodyIndex]
+                                       : make_double4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
+
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += WARP_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + warpLane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            warpTile[warpLane] = oldPositions[sourceIndex];
+        }
+        __syncwarp();
+
+        const int tileCount = min(WARP_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+#pragma unroll 8
+        for (int source = 0; source < tileCount; ++source) {
+            const double4 otherPosition = warpTile[source];
+            const double dx = otherPosition.x - ownPosition.x;
+            const double dy = otherPosition.y - ownPosition.y;
+            const double dz = otherPosition.z - ownPosition.z;
+            const double distanceSquared =
+                dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+            // CUDA's correctly rounded reciprocal-square-root avoids issuing
+            // separate square-root and division instructions.
+            const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+            const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+            forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncwarp();
     }
+
+    if (active) {
+        double4 velocity = velocities[bodyIndex];
+        velocity.x += DT * forceX;
+        velocity.y += DT * forceY;
+        velocity.z += DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocities[bodyIndex] = velocity;
+        newPositions[bodyIndex] =
+            make_double4(ownPosition.x + velocity.x * DT,
+                         ownPosition.y + velocity.y * DT,
+                         ownPosition.z + velocity.z * DT,
+                         0.0);
+    }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {

+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : numBodies_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            positions[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].pos.x, bodies[i].pos.y,
+                                        bodies[i].pos.z, 0.0);
+            velocities[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].vel.x, bodies[i].vel.y,
+                                         bodies[i].vel.z, 0.0);
+        }
+
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[0], bytes), "position allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[1], bytes), "position scratch allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&velocities_, bytes), "velocity allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_[0], positions.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial positions to the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, velocities.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial velocities to the GPU");
+
+        // Force lazy CUDA module loading to happen during backend setup rather
+        // than on the first timed simulation step.  With zero bodies the
+        // kernel only traverses its synchronization-safe inactive path.
+        advanceBodies<CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE><<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            positions_[0], positions_[1], velocities_, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "warming up the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "warming up the CUDA device");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(positions_[0]);
+        cudaFree(positions_[1]);
+        cudaFree(velocities_);
+    }
+
+    void run(const int numSteps) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0 || numSteps == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int gridSize =
+            (numBodies_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            advanceBodies<CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE><<<gridSize, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+                positions_[currentPositions_],
+                positions_[currentPositions_ ^ 1], velocities_, numBodies_);
+            currentPositions_ ^= 1;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the n-body simulation");
+    }
+
+    void copyToHost(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions.data(), positions_[currentPositions_],
+                             bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final positions from the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities.data(), velocities_, bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final velocities from the GPU");
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(positions[i].x, positions[i].y, positions[i].z);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(velocities[i].x, velocities[i].y,
+                                 velocities[i].z);
+        }
     }
-}
 
+  private:
+    int numBodies_ = 0;
+    int currentPositions_ = 0;
+    double4* positions_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    double4* velocities_ = nullptr;
+};

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+inline void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        cudaFailure(status, operation);
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+
+// Each one-warp block streams a coalesced position tile through shared memory.
+// Warp-level barriers are cheaper than whole-block barriers, while shared-memory
+// broadcasts reuse every source load for 32 target bodies.  Positions are
+// written to a different allocation, so every force in a step observes exactly
+// the same pre-integration snapshot.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void advanceBodies(const double4* __restrict__ oldPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ newPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ velocities,
+                   const int numBodies) {
+    __shared__ double4 positionTile[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int bodyIndex = blockIdx.x * CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE + lane;
+    const bool active = bodyIndex < numBodies;
+    const double4 ownPosition = active ? oldPositions[bodyIndex]
+                                       : make_double4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
+
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += WARP_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + warpLane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            positionTile[lane] = oldPositions[sourceIndex];
+        }
+        __syncwarp();
+
+        const int tileCount = min(WARP_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+#pragma unroll 8
+        for (int source = 0; source < tileCount; ++source) {
+            const double4 otherPosition = positionTile[source];
+            const double dx = otherPosition.x - ownPosition.x;
+            const double dy = otherPosition.y - ownPosition.y;
+            const double dz = otherPosition.z - ownPosition.z;
+            const double distanceSquared =
+                dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+            // CUDA's correctly rounded reciprocal-square-root avoids issuing
+            // separate square-root and division instructions.
+            const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+            const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+            forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncwarp();
+    }
+
+    if (active) {
+        double4 velocity = velocities[bodyIndex];
+        velocity.x += DT * forceX;
+        velocity.y += DT * forceY;
+        velocity.z += DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocities[bodyIndex] = velocity;
+        newPositions[bodyIndex] =
+            make_double4(ownPosition.x + velocity.x * DT,
+                         ownPosition.y + velocity.y * DT,
+                         ownPosition.z + velocity.z * DT,
+                         0.0);
     }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : numBodies_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            positions[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].pos.x, bodies[i].pos.y,
+                                        bodies[i].pos.z, 0.0);
+            velocities[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].vel.x, bodies[i].vel.y,
+                                         bodies[i].vel.z, 0.0);
+        }
+
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[0], bytes), "position allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[1], bytes), "position scratch allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&velocities_, bytes), "velocity allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_[0], positions.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial positions to the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, velocities.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial velocities to the GPU");
+
+        // Force lazy CUDA module loading to happen during backend setup rather
+        // than on the first timed simulation step.  With zero bodies the
+        // kernel only traverses its synchronization-safe inactive path.
+        advanceBodies<<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            positions_[0], positions_[1], velocities_, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "warming up the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "warming up the CUDA device");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(positions_[0]);
+        cudaFree(positions_[1]);
+        cudaFree(velocities_);
+    }
+
+    void run(const int numSteps) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0 || numSteps == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int gridSize =
+            (numBodies_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            advanceBodies<<<gridSize, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+                positions_[currentPositions_],
+                positions_[currentPositions_ ^ 1], velocities_, numBodies_);
+            currentPositions_ ^= 1;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the n-body simulation");
     }
-}
 
+    void copyToHost(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }

+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions.data(), positions_[currentPositions_],
+                             bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),

+        }
+    }

+  private:
+    int numBodies_ = 0;
+    int currentPositions_ = 0;

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+inline void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        cudaFailure(status, operation);
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+
+// Each one-warp block streams a coalesced position tile through shared memory.
+// Warp-level barriers are cheaper than whole-block barriers, while shared-memory
+// broadcasts reuse every source load for 32 target bodies.  Positions are
+// written to a different allocation, so every force in a step observes exactly
+// the same pre-integration snapshot.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void advanceBodies(const double4* __restrict__ oldPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ newPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ velocities,
+                   const int numBodies) {
+    __shared__ double4 positionTile[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int bodyIndex = blockIdx.x * CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE + lane;
+    const bool active = bodyIndex < numBodies;
+    const double4 ownPosition = active ? oldPositions[bodyIndex]
+                                       : make_double4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
+
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += WARP_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            positionTile[lane] = oldPositions[sourceIndex];
+        }
+        __syncwarp();
+
+        const int tileCount = min(WARP_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+#pragma unroll 8
+        for (int source = 0; source < tileCount; ++source) {
+            const double4 otherPosition = positionTile[source];
+            const double dx = otherPosition.x - ownPosition.x;
+            const double dy = otherPosition.y - ownPosition.y;
+            const double dz = otherPosition.z - ownPosition.z;
+            const double distanceSquared =
+                dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+            // CUDA's correctly rounded reciprocal-square-root avoids issuing
+            // separate square-root and division instructions.
+            const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+            const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+            forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncwarp();
+    }
+
+    if (active) {
+        double4 velocity = velocities[bodyIndex];
+        velocity.x += DT * forceX;
+        velocity.y += DT * forceY;
+        velocity.z += DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocities[bodyIndex] = velocity;
+        newPositions[bodyIndex] =
+            make_double4(ownPosition.x + velocity.x * DT,
+                         ownPosition.y + velocity.y * DT,
+                         ownPosition.z + velocity.z * DT,
+                         0.0);
     }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;

+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : numBodies_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            positions[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].pos.x, bodies[i].pos.y,
+                                        bodies[i].pos.z, 0.0);
+            velocities[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].vel.x, bodies[i].vel.y,
+                                         bodies[i].vel.z, 0.0);
+        }
+
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[0], bytes), "position allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[1], bytes), "position scratch allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&velocities_, bytes), "velocity allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_[0], positions.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial positions to the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, velocities.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial velocities to the GPU");
+
+        // Force lazy CUDA module loading to happen during backend setup rather
+        // than on the first timed simulation step.  With zero bodies the
+        // kernel only traverses its synchronization-safe inactive path.
+        advanceBodies<<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            positions_[0], positions_[1], velocities_, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "warming up the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "warming up the CUDA device");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(positions_[0]);
+        cudaFree(positions_[1]);
+        cudaFree(velocities_);
+    }
+
+    void run(const int numSteps) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0 || numSteps == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int gridSize =
+            (numBodies_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            advanceBodies<<<gridSize, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+                positions_[currentPositions_],
+                positions_[currentPositions_ ^ 1], velocities_, numBodies_);
+            currentPositions_ ^= 1;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the n-body simulation");
     }
-}
 
+    void copyToHost(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions.data(), positions_[currentPositions_],
+                             bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final positions from the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities.data(), velocities_, bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final velocities from the GPU");
+

+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(positions[i].x, positions[i].y, positions[i].z);

+                                 velocities[i].z);
+        }
+    }
+
+  private:
+    int numBodies_ = 0;
+    int currentPositions_ = 0;
+    double4* positions_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    double4* velocities_ = nullptr;
+};

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);

+inline void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        cudaFailure(status, operation);
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+
+// Each one-warp block streams a coalesced position tile through shared memory.
+// Warp-level barriers are cheaper than whole-block barriers, while shared-memory
+// broadcasts reuse every source load for 32 target bodies.  Positions are
+// written to a different allocation, so every force in a step observes exactly
+// the same pre-integration snapshot.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void advanceBodies(const double4* __restrict__ oldPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ newPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ velocities,
+                   const int numBodies) {
+    __shared__ double4 positionTile[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int bodyIndex = blockIdx.x * CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE + lane;
+    const bool active = bodyIndex < numBodies;
+    const double4 ownPosition = active ? oldPositions[bodyIndex]
+                                       : make_double4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
+
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += WARP_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            positionTile[lane] = oldPositions[sourceIndex];
+        }
+        __syncwarp();
+
+        const int tileCount = min(WARP_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+#pragma unroll 8
+        for (int source = 0; source < tileCount; ++source) {
+            const double4 otherPosition = positionTile[source];
+            const double dx = otherPosition.x - ownPosition.x;
+            const double dy = otherPosition.y - ownPosition.y;
+            const double dz = otherPosition.z - ownPosition.z;
+            const double distanceSquared =
+                dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+            // A direct reciprocal square root avoids issuing separate square-
+            // root and division operations while retaining double precision.
+            const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+            const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+            forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncwarp();
+    }
+
+    if (active) {
+        double4 velocity = velocities[bodyIndex];
+        velocity.x += DT * forceX;
+        velocity.y += DT * forceY;
+        velocity.z += DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocities[bodyIndex] = velocity;
+        newPositions[bodyIndex] =
+            make_double4(ownPosition.x + velocity.x * DT,
+                         ownPosition.y + velocity.y * DT,
+                         ownPosition.z + velocity.z * DT,
+                         0.0);
     }
 }

+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : numBodies_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            positions[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].pos.x, bodies[i].pos.y,
+                                        bodies[i].pos.z, 0.0);
+            velocities[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].vel.x, bodies[i].vel.y,
+                                         bodies[i].vel.z, 0.0);
+        }
+
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[0], bytes), "position allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[1], bytes), "position scratch allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&velocities_, bytes), "velocity allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_[0], positions.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial positions to the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, velocities.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial velocities to the GPU");
+
+        // Force lazy CUDA module loading to happen during backend setup rather
+        // than on the first timed simulation step.  With zero bodies the
+        // kernel only traverses its synchronization-safe inactive path.
+        advanceBodies<<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            positions_[0], positions_[1], velocities_, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "warming up the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "warming up the CUDA device");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(positions_[0]);
+        cudaFree(positions_[1]);
+        cudaFree(velocities_);
+    }
+
+    void run(const int numSteps) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0 || numSteps == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int gridSize =
+            (numBodies_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            advanceBodies<<<gridSize, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+                positions_[currentPositions_],
+                positions_[currentPositions_ ^ 1], velocities_, numBodies_);
+            currentPositions_ ^= 1;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the n-body simulation");
     }
-}

+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());

+        }
+    }
+
+  private:
+    int numBodies_ = 0;
+    int currentPositions_ = 0;
+    double4* positions_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};

+};

+// Warp-level barriers are cheaper than whole-block barriers, while shared-memory

+void advanceBodies(const double4* __restrict__ oldPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ newPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ velocities,
+                   const int numBodies) {
+    __shared__ double4 positionTile[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int bodyIndex = blockIdx.x * CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE + lane;
+    const bool active = bodyIndex < numBodies;
+    const double4 ownPosition = active ? oldPositions[bodyIndex]
+                                       : make_double4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
+

+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += WARP_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            positionTile[lane] = oldPositions[sourceIndex];
+        }
+        __syncwarp();
+
+        const int tileCount = min(WARP_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);

+        for (int source = 0; source < tileCount; ++source) {
+            const double4 otherPosition = positionTile[source];
+            const double dx = otherPosition.x - ownPosition.x;
+            const double dy = otherPosition.y - ownPosition.y;
+            const double dz = otherPosition.z - ownPosition.z;
+            const double distanceSquared =

+            const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+

+        velocity.y += DT * forceY;

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+inline void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        cudaFailure(status, operation);
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+
+// Each one-warp block streams a coalesced position tile through shared memory.
+// Warp-level barriers are cheaper than whole-block barriers, while shared-memory
+// broadcasts reuse every source load for 32 target bodies.  Positions are
+// written to a different allocation, so every force in a step observes exactly
+// the same pre-integration snapshot.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void advanceBodies(const double4* __restrict__ oldPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ newPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ velocities,
+                   const int numBodies) {
+    __shared__ double4 positionTile[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int bodyIndex = blockIdx.x * CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE + lane;
+    const bool active = bodyIndex < numBodies;
+    const double4 ownPosition = active ? oldPositions[bodyIndex]
+                                       : make_double4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
+
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += WARP_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            positionTile[lane] = oldPositions[sourceIndex];
+        }
+        __syncwarp();
+
+        const int tileCount = min(WARP_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+#pragma unroll 8
+        for (int source = 0; source < tileCount; ++source) {
+            const double4 otherPosition = positionTile[source];
+            const double dx = otherPosition.x - ownPosition.x;
+            const double dy = otherPosition.y - ownPosition.y;
+            const double dz = otherPosition.z - ownPosition.z;
+            const double distanceSquared =
+                dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+            // A direct reciprocal square root avoids issuing separate square-
+            // root and division operations while retaining double precision.
+            const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+            const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+            forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncwarp();
+    }
+
+    if (active) {
+        double4 velocity = velocities[bodyIndex];
+        velocity.x += DT * forceX;
+        velocity.y += DT * forceY;
+        velocity.z += DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocities[bodyIndex] = velocity;
+        newPositions[bodyIndex] =
+            make_double4(ownPosition.x + velocity.x * DT,
+                         ownPosition.y + velocity.y * DT,
+                         ownPosition.z + velocity.z * DT,
+                         0.0);
     }
 }

+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : numBodies_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            positions[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].pos.x, bodies[i].pos.y,
+                                        bodies[i].pos.z, 0.0);
+            velocities[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].vel.x, bodies[i].vel.y,
+                                         bodies[i].vel.z, 0.0);
+        }
+
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[0], bytes), "position allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[1], bytes), "position scratch allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&velocities_, bytes), "velocity allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_[0], positions.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial positions to the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, velocities.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial velocities to the GPU");
+
+        // Force lazy CUDA module loading to happen during backend setup rather
+        // than on the first timed simulation step.  With zero bodies the
+        // kernel only traverses its synchronization-safe inactive path.
+        advanceBodies<<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            positions_[0], positions_[1], velocities_, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "warming up the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "warming up the CUDA device");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(positions_[0]);
+        cudaFree(positions_[1]);
+        cudaFree(velocities_);
+    }
+
+    void run(const int numSteps) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0 || numSteps == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int gridSize =
+            (numBodies_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            advanceBodies<<<gridSize, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+                positions_[currentPositions_],
+                positions_[currentPositions_ ^ 1], velocities_, numBodies_);
+            currentPositions_ ^= 1;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the n-body simulation");
     }
-}
 
+    void copyToHost(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions.data(), positions_[currentPositions_],
+                             bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final positions from the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities.data(), velocities_, bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final velocities from the GPU");
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(positions[i].x, positions[i].y, positions[i].z);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(velocities[i].x, velocities[i].y,
+                                 velocities[i].z);
+        }
+    }
+
+  private:
+    int numBodies_ = 0;
+    int currentPositions_ = 0;
+    double4* positions_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    double4* velocities_ = nullptr;
+};

codex
The final tuned kernel is now one warp per block with warp-level barriers, shared-memory source broadcasts, FP64 reciprocal-square-root, and fused force/integration over ping-pong position buffers. On this RTX 3090, the default case dropped from the original CPU’s 77 ms to about 3 ms while preserving validation and closely matching reference samples; an awkward 1,025-body case also passes. I’m doing the final clean-build and CUDA memory/race checks now.

+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[1], bytes), "position scratch allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&velocities_, bytes), "velocity allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_[0], positions.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial positions to the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, velocities.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial velocities to the GPU");
+
+        // Force lazy CUDA module loading to happen during backend setup rather
+        // than on the first timed simulation step.  With zero bodies the
+        // kernel only traverses its synchronization-safe inactive path.
+        advanceBodies<<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            positions_[0], positions_[1], velocities_, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "warming up the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "warming up the CUDA device");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(positions_[0]);
+        cudaFree(positions_[1]);
+        cudaFree(velocities_);
+    }
+
+    void run(const int numSteps) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0 || numSteps == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int gridSize =
+            (numBodies_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            advanceBodies<<<gridSize, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+                positions_[currentPositions_],
+                positions_[currentPositions_ ^ 1], velocities_, numBodies_);
+            currentPositions_ ^= 1;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the n-body simulation");
+    }
+
+    void copyToHost(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions.data(), positions_[currentPositions_],
+                             bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final positions from the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities.data(), velocities_, bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final velocities from the GPU");
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(positions[i].x, positions[i].y, positions[i].z);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(velocities[i].x, velocities[i].y,
+                                 velocities[i].z);
+        }
+    }
+
+  private:
+    int numBodies_ = 0;
+    int currentPositions_ = 0;
+    double4* positions_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    double4* velocities_ = nullptr;
+};

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+inline void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        cudaFailure(status, operation);
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+
+// Each one-warp block streams a coalesced position tile through shared memory.
+// Warp-level barriers are cheaper than whole-block barriers, while shared-memory
+// broadcasts reuse every source load for 32 target bodies.  Positions are
+// written to a different allocation, so every force in a step observes exactly
+// the same pre-integration snapshot.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void advanceBodies(const double4* __restrict__ oldPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ newPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ velocities,
+                   const int numBodies) {
+    __shared__ double4 positionTile[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int bodyIndex = blockIdx.x * CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE + lane;
+    const bool active = bodyIndex < numBodies;
+    const double4 ownPosition = active ? oldPositions[bodyIndex]
+                                       : make_double4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
+
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += WARP_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            positionTile[lane] = oldPositions[sourceIndex];
+        }
+        __syncwarp();
+
+        const int tileCount = min(WARP_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+#pragma unroll 8
+        for (int source = 0; source < tileCount; ++source) {
+            const double4 otherPosition = positionTile[source];
+            const double dx = otherPosition.x - ownPosition.x;
+            const double dy = otherPosition.y - ownPosition.y;
+            const double dz = otherPosition.z - ownPosition.z;
+            const double distanceSquared =
+                dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+            // A direct reciprocal square root avoids issuing separate square-
+            // root and division operations while retaining double precision.
+            const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+            const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+            forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncwarp();
     }
-}
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+    if (active) {
+        double4 velocity = velocities[bodyIndex];
+        velocity.x += DT * forceX;
+        velocity.y += DT * forceY;
+        velocity.z += DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocities[bodyIndex] = velocity;
+        newPositions[bodyIndex] =
+            make_double4(ownPosition.x + velocity.x * DT,
+                         ownPosition.y + velocity.y * DT,
+                         ownPosition.z + velocity.z * DT,
+                         0.0);
     }
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : numBodies_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            positions[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].pos.x, bodies[i].pos.y,
+                                        bodies[i].pos.z, 0.0);
+            velocities[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].vel.x, bodies[i].vel.y,
+                                         bodies[i].vel.z, 0.0);
+        }
+
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[0], bytes), "position allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[1], bytes), "position scratch allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&velocities_, bytes), "velocity allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_[0], positions.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial positions to the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, velocities.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial velocities to the GPU");
+
+        // Force lazy CUDA module loading to happen during backend setup rather
+        // than on the first timed simulation step.  With zero bodies the
+        // kernel only traverses its synchronization-safe inactive path.
+        advanceBodies<<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            positions_[0], positions_[1], velocities_, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "warming up the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "warming up the CUDA device");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(positions_[0]);
+        cudaFree(positions_[1]);
+        cudaFree(velocities_);
+    }
+
+    void run(const int numSteps) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0 || numSteps == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int gridSize =
+            (numBodies_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            advanceBodies<<<gridSize, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+                positions_[currentPositions_],
+                positions_[currentPositions_ ^ 1], velocities_, numBodies_);
+            currentPositions_ ^= 1;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the n-body simulation");
+    }
+
+    void copyToHost(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions.data(), positions_[currentPositions_],
+                             bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final positions from the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities.data(), velocities_, bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final velocities from the GPU");
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(positions[i].x, positions[i].y, positions[i].z);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(velocities[i].x, velocities[i].y,
+                                 velocities[i].z);
+        }
+    }
+
+  private:
+    int numBodies_ = 0;
+    int currentPositions_ = 0;
+    double4* positions_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    double4* velocities_ = nullptr;
+};

+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[1], bytes), "position scratch allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&velocities_, bytes), "velocity allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_[0], positions.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial positions to the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, velocities.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial velocities to the GPU");
+
+        // Force lazy CUDA module loading to happen during backend setup rather
+        // than on the first timed simulation step.  With zero bodies the
+        // kernel only traverses its synchronization-safe inactive path.
+        advanceBodies<<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            positions_[0], positions_[1], velocities_, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "warming up the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "warming up the CUDA device");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(positions_[0]);
+        cudaFree(positions_[1]);
+        cudaFree(velocities_);
+    }

+    void run(const int numSteps) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0 || numSteps == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int gridSize =
+            (numBodies_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            advanceBodies<<<gridSize, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+                positions_[currentPositions_],
+                positions_[currentPositions_ ^ 1], velocities_, numBodies_);
+            currentPositions_ ^= 1;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the n-body simulation");
+    }
+

+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);

+                                 velocities[i].z);
+        }
+    }

+  private:
+    int numBodies_ = 0;
+    int currentPositions_ = 0;
+    double4* positions_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    double4* velocities_ = nullptr;
+};

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+inline void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        cudaFailure(status, operation);
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+
+// Each one-warp block streams a coalesced position tile through shared memory.
+// Warp-level barriers are cheaper than whole-block barriers, while shared-memory
+// broadcasts reuse every source load for 32 target bodies.  Positions are
+// written to a different allocation, so every force in a step observes exactly
+// the same pre-integration snapshot.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void advanceBodies(const double4* __restrict__ oldPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ newPositions,
+                   double4* __restrict__ velocities,
+                   const int numBodies) {
+    __shared__ double4 positionTile[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int bodyIndex = blockIdx.x * CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE + lane;
+    const bool active = bodyIndex < numBodies;
+    const double4 ownPosition = active ? oldPositions[bodyIndex]
+                                       : make_double4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
+
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += WARP_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            positionTile[lane] = oldPositions[sourceIndex];
+        }
+        __syncwarp();
+
+        const int tileCount = min(WARP_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+#pragma unroll 8
+        for (int source = 0; source < tileCount; ++source) {
+            const double4 otherPosition = positionTile[source];
+            const double dx = otherPosition.x - ownPosition.x;
+            const double dy = otherPosition.y - ownPosition.y;
+            const double dz = otherPosition.z - ownPosition.z;
+            const double distanceSquared =
+                dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+            // A direct reciprocal square root avoids issuing separate square-
+            // root and division operations while retaining double precision.
+            const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+            const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+            forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncwarp();
     }
-}
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+    if (active) {
+        double4 velocity = velocities[bodyIndex];
+        velocity.x += DT * forceX;
+        velocity.y += DT * forceY;
+        velocity.z += DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocities[bodyIndex] = velocity;
+        newPositions[bodyIndex] =
+            make_double4(ownPosition.x + velocity.x * DT,
+                         ownPosition.y + velocity.y * DT,
+                         ownPosition.z + velocity.z * DT,
+                         0.0);
     }
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : numBodies_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            positions[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].pos.x, bodies[i].pos.y,
+                                        bodies[i].pos.z, 0.0);
+            velocities[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].vel.x, bodies[i].vel.y,
+                                         bodies[i].vel.z, 0.0);
+        }
+
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[0], bytes), "position allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[1], bytes), "position scratch allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&velocities_, bytes), "velocity allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_[0], positions.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial positions to the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, velocities.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial velocities to the GPU");

+    }

+    double forceZ = 0.0;

+            const double dy = otherPosition.y - ownPosition.y;
+            const double dz = otherPosition.z - ownPosition.z;

+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : numBodies_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            positions[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].pos.x, bodies[i].pos.y,
+                                        bodies[i].pos.z, 0.0);
+            velocities[i] = make_double4(bodies[i].vel.x, bodies[i].vel.y,
+                                         bodies[i].vel.z, 0.0);
+        }
+
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[0], bytes), "position allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&positions_[1], bytes), "position scratch allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&velocities_, bytes), "velocity allocation");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_[0], positions.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial positions to the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, velocities.data(), bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying initial velocities to the GPU");
+
+        // Force lazy CUDA module loading to happen during backend setup rather
+        // than on the first timed simulation step.  With zero bodies the
+        // kernel only traverses its synchronization-safe inactive path.
+        advanceBodies<<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            positions_[0], positions_[1], velocities_, 0);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "warming up the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "warming up the CUDA device");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(positions_[0]);
+        cudaFree(positions_[1]);
+        cudaFree(velocities_);
+    }
+
+    void run(const int numSteps) {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0 || numSteps == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int gridSize =
+            (numBodies_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            advanceBodies<<<gridSize, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+                positions_[currentPositions_],
+                positions_[currentPositions_ ^ 1], velocities_, numBodies_);
+            currentPositions_ ^= 1;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the n-body kernel");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the n-body simulation");
+    }
+
+    void copyToHost(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (numBodies_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        std::vector<double4> positions(bodies.size());
+        std::vector<double4> velocities(bodies.size());
+        const size_t bytes = bodies.size() * sizeof(double4);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions.data(), positions_[currentPositions_],
+                             bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final positions from the GPU");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities.data(), velocities_, bytes,
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copying final velocities from the GPU");
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(positions[i].x, positions[i].y, positions[i].z);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(velocities[i].x, velocities[i].y,
+                                 velocities[i].z);
+        }
+    }
+
+  private:
+    int numBodies_ = 0;
+    int currentPositions_ = 0;
+    double4* positions_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    double4* velocities_ = nullptr;
+};
